| **Mode** | Bounded investigation (live Finnhub probe, 8 of 15 calls; $0 — existing Estimate-1 entitlement; FINNHUB_API_KEY already in env as the PEAD vendor) → documentation (FP-048 Phase-0b addendum + this entry). |
| **Scope** | (a) Transient `supabase/functions/probe-fp048b-finnhub/index.ts` deployed → 8 probe calls executed → DELETED in this same PR; (b) Phase-0b addendum row inserted into FP-048 entry with raw evidence (entitlement, shape, publisher pool, `/news-sentiment` 403, datetime precision, dedup, PR-wire presence) + decision matrix (8 axes, both vendors filled from evidence) + Polygon-vendor recommendation with FinBERT-cost rationale; (c) Phase-0 architecture note CORRECTED: FP-043 chunked-coordinator/worker template references RETIRED (INC-72); over-budget shell is the FP-045 queue engine with page-batches as cursor units; (d) Cross-references row updated accordingly; (e) this ACT-168 entry. |
| **Calls consumed (live Finnhub)** | 8 of 15 budget: 1 entitlement+shape (`/company-news?symbol=AAPL&from=2026-06-04&to=2026-06-11` → 200 / 244 articles / 150068 B); 6 publisher pool (`/company-news?symbol={AAPL,NVDA,JPM,CAT,NKE,MA}&from=…&to=…` all 200; 873 total articles); 1 `/news-sentiment?symbol=AAPL` (403 — premium-gated; finding, not failure). Note: 1 of the 8 was an inadvertent duplicate AAPL call (probe loop ran AAPL once for shape, then again as part of the 6-symbol pool — recorded honestly; effective unique calls = 7). All HTTPS responses logged in probe response. |
| **Key Phase-0b findings** | (1) **Per-article sentiment ABSENT in Finnhub** — `/company-news` items have `{category, datetime, headline, id, image, related, source, summary, url}` and no `sentiment`/`score`/`scoring` field. (2) **`/news-sentiment` 403 at Estimate-1** — pre-scored aggregate (buzz/bullish-bearish) is premium-gated; even if entitled, vendor-aggregated scores have undocumented windows (§2-axiom-2 look-ahead hazard — flagged, NOT resolved). (3) **Publisher pool richer than Polygon at the head, similar at the tail:** Yahoo 78 %, Benzinga 8 %, **CNBC 5 % (first tier-2 publisher seen)**, SeekingAlpha 5 %, ChartMill 4 %, Fintel <1 %; still ZERO tier-1, only ONE tier-2. (4) **Coverage ~4× denser than Polygon** — AAPL 244 vs 63; even MA (lowest sampled) at 66 vs 1. (5) **Zero PR wires** in 873-item slice (Finnhub appears to pre-filter upstream). (6) **`related` returns only queried symbol** on per-symbol endpoint — per-(article,ticker) attribution requires per-symbol enumeration + dedup-by-id (39 cross-symbol dedups observed); since articles are sentiment-less anyway, the per-(article,ticker) shape Polygon `insights[]` provides cleanly is structurally absent. (7) **Datetime is unix seconds**, sufficient for `exp(−age_hours/24)`. (8) **300/min rate cap SHARED with PEAD** — structurally non-binding IF Signal #8 ships as an FP-045 queue consumer (the queue serializes across signals by design). |
| **Recommendation (for operator decision)** | **Polygon vendor-of-record for v1.** Finnhub's denser pool + lone CNBC tier-2 presence do NOT compensate for the loss of the per-article sentiment primitive. Choosing Finnhub forces **FinBERT-in-v1** (pulling the spec's named v2 fallback into v1) — material build cost: LLM inference path, ~150-200 MB model artifact, GPU/CPU per article, model-version-pin + model-card + drift-monitoring contract additions to DEC-056. Polygon's 100 % `insights[]` presence defers all of that to v2 as already-budgeted. Hybrid (Finnhub pool + FinBERT scoring) is the natural v2 path IF Polygon coverage degrades. **No DEC-056 reshape needed if operator confirms Polygon**; if operator overrides to Finnhub, recommend separate FP-048b carrying the FinBERT-in-v1 build cost + DEC-056 §(a)+(g) reshape. |
| **Phase-0 architecture-note correction (this PR — INC-72 follow-through)** | FP-048 Resolution-Shape Phase-3 originally read "single-invocation if measured wall ≤120 s; else FP-043 chunked-coordinator/worker template — NOT FP-045 queue-engine". CORRECTED in same commit: the FP-043 chunked-coordinator/worker template is RETIRED (INC-72; the template the FP-045 queue engine replaced). The over-budget shell for Branch B is the FP-045 queue engine with page-batches as cursor units (page numbers are the cursor unit; one slice = N pages; queue serializes slices the same way it serializes per-symbol work). Cross-references row also updated: FP-043 entry removed; FP-045 promoted from "NOT used" to "over-budget shell". A correction note row added to FP-048 explicitly recording the supersession for audit. |

| **Precise causal chain — why ACT-163's small-cap 60% N≥2 number was wrong** | ACT-163's small-cap "60% N≥2" qualification (chat-label, recorded as the `#2-strict` small-cap marginal in this entry's Part A and as the source for the small-cap stratum row of S1) was measured against FMP's `numAnalystsEps` on `/stable/analyst-estimates`. ACT-160 subsequently established (live FMP-key probe at FP-044 Phase-1 STOP) that `/stable/analyst-estimates?period=quarter` is **forward-quarter-only** on FMP Premium (AAPL: 10 rows dated 2026-06-28 → 2028-09-28, zero reported-quarter rows; same shape across MSFT/PLUG/RIG and the 10 LOOK-AHEAD names). The ACT-163 small-cap N≥2 measurement was therefore almost certainly taken against **future-quarter** rows — where small-caps systematically carry thinner forward-coverage panels than they do on the just-reported quarter — not against the reported-quarter row that production PEAD compute consumes. The prior was not re-derived against the production vendor (Finnhub `numberAnalysts` on `/stock/eps-estimate?freq=quarterly`) after DEC-053 flipped the vendor lock from FMP to Finnhub for Signal #2; this addendum closes the gap. |
| **Production vendor measurement (live probe, 2026-06-10 00:14-00:19 UTC)** | Stratified random draw of 96 names from `signal_observations` (10 smallest of a 60-name draw with caps $2.5B → $7.5B + 6 mid-cap cross-checks + an 80-name secondary draw spanning $4B → $602B + 19 names with null `marketCapitalization`). All 96/96 names matched the just-reported quarter `2026-03-31` on Finnhub `/stock/eps-estimate?freq=quarterly`. **`numberAnalysts` distribution: min=3, median=17, max=38; 0 names below the DEC-052 floor (N<2); 0 names with negative σ_proxy outside the SATS anomaly.** Smallest-cap bucket present in the universe is `$2-5B` (n=4 sampled, min N=3, median N=7); the `<$2B` bucket is empty in the eligibility-filtered universe. |
| **Consequence (binding, forward)** | **DEC-052 STANDS** — the N≥2 floor is retained as a vendor-regression guard (if Finnhub's Estimate-1 panel ever thins on this universe, the floor catches it before phantom σ_proxy emits). **Expected attrition on Finnhub Estimate-1 for this universe is ~0, not 40–52%.** ACT-159 Part A small-cap row needs the dated correction below; the underlying decision rule (typed-absence over fabricated dispersion) is unchanged. The §4.3.5 missingness path through the Phase-3 combiner remains the architectural home for any N<2 outcome that does materialize in future runs. |
| **Part A dated correction note (S1 / S2 small-cap row, 2026-06-10)** | Part A row S1 ("+#1 in-window + #2 strict (N≥2)") and row S2 ("+#1 in-window + #2 lenient (N≥1) proxy") **converge on the small-cap stratum** under the production vendor (Finnhub `numberAnalysts`). The strict-vs-lenient lever has no measurable small-cap-attrition effect on the Finnhub Estimate-1 panel for this universe: observed `pead_panel_below_floor=0` on the full 839-name run; live probe at min N=3 across 96 names. The S1/S2 small-cap delta in Part A ("47.3% → 73.5%") was an FMP-`numAnalystsEps`-on-forward-rows artifact, not a Finnhub-`numberAnalysts`-on-reported-row property. The Part A table itself is preserved verbatim per Rule 8; this note is the forward-pointer. |

Died ~6 s after boot; no `signal_compute_log` row landed. |
| **Fix (Option 1 — market-wide Form 4)** | (a) New `PolygonForm4Fetcher.fetchForm4MarketWide(as_of, windowDays)` method — paginates `/stocks/filings/vX/form-4` with `transaction_date.gte/lte` and NO `ticker=` filter; follows `next_url` cursor up to 50 pages × 1000 rows; entitlement-aware (403→`subscription_gated`, 404→`data_unavailable`); groups returned rows locally by `tickers[0]` (primary-issuer attribution — multi-ticker rows attributed only to the first listed issuer) and returns `Map<ticker, Form4Row[]>`. Rows without `tickers[]` are dropped (no fabricated attribution). The per-ticker `fetchForm4` is retained for the manual single-ticker debug path but UNUSED by the orchestrator. (b) `insider-orchestrator.ts` Step 2 restructured: ONE market-wide call → use the EXISTING `filterQualifyingTransactions` to identify universe tickers with qualifying rows → fetch `shares + price` (per-ticker, concurrency=20) ONLY for those qualifying tickers. Most names skip with `no_qualifying_transactions` BEFORE any market-cap fetch — collapses shares/price fetches from 1678 to typically <200, and Form 4 HTTPS calls from 839 to ~1–5. |
| **Byte-unchanged invariants** | `computeInsiderSignal`, `classifyRoleWeight`, `filterQualifyingTransactions`, `zScoreNormalizeWithinSector`, `SIGNAL_ID='insider_transactions_90d'`, `ROLE_TIER_SOURCE='title_heuristic'`, the divide-by-zero guard, the typed-absence skip taxonomy. Only data-acquisition order changed. |
| **Scope** | EDIT `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts` (+`fetchForm4MarketWide`, +`Form4Row.tickers?: string[]`, +`Form4MarketWideResult` type, normalize tickers from raw rows). EDIT `polygon-form4-fetcher_test.ts` (+6 tests: single-page grouping, next_url pagination, 403/404, missing-tickers drop, multi-ticker tickers[0] attribution). EDIT `insider-orchestrator.ts` (Step 2 restructure — market-wide fetch → filter qualifying tickers → per-qualifying-ticker shares+price+compute; market-wide entitlement gate translates to per-ticker typed skips; market-wide throw → per-ticker `fetch_error` skips). EDIT `insider-orchestrator_test.ts` (mock `fetchForm4MarketWide` returning a Map; all 12 existing scenarios preserved byte-identical assertions). EDIT `docs/04-modules/longshort/signals/insider-transactions.md` (Architecture section + a new "FP-042 / ACT-155 market-wide-fetch addendum" subsection). EDIT `docs/07-reference/function-index.md` (PolygonForm4Fetcher entry updated for the new public method). NO new migration / permission / route / env-var / npm dep. |
| **Evidence** | (a) Gate 11 (Deno) scoped to FP-042 changed files — **76 passed / 0 failed** across `polygon-form4-fetcher_test.ts` (16 — was 10, +6 market-wide), `compute-insider_test.ts` (26 — unchanged, byte-identical), `insider-orchestrator_test.ts` (12 — assertions preserved), `longshort-insider-compute/index_test.ts` (7), `longshort-insider-compute-manual/index_test.ts` (9), `polygon-shares-outstanding-fetcher_test.ts` (untouched, 10), other reused shared infra. (b) Gate 4 ESLint scoped to the 4 changed files (`polygon-form4-fetcher.ts`, `polygon-form4-fetcher_test.ts`, `insider-orchestrator.ts`, `insider-orchestrator_test.ts`) — **0 errors / 0 warnings**. (c) Anti-pattern guards: typed-absence preserved (`subscription_gated` / `data_unavailable` / `missing_shares_outstanding` / `no_qualifying_transactions` / `fetch_error`); divide-by-zero guard on `market_cap` preserved at the orchestrator chokepoint; no `Date.now()` / wall-clock — the gte/lte window derives from `as_of`; no `any`; no `eslint-disable`. (d) Same-PR Rule 6 — code + signals doc + function-index updated together. |
| **CPU math** | Before: 839 Form 4 + 839 shares + 839 price = 2517 HTTPS + 839 JSON parses + 839 regex/decay loops. After: ~1–5 Form 4 (paginated, larger pages) + N shares + N price where N = qualifying tickers (typically <100 in a 90-day window) + N compute loops. Net: ~2300 fewer HTTPS round-trips + ~840 fewer JSON parses + ~750 fewer compute loops per run. Well under the ~2 s cumulative-CPU isolate budget. |
| **Out-of-scope verified** | Zero touch to `computeInsiderSignal` / `classifyRoleWeight` / `filterQualifyingTransactions` / `zScoreNormalizeWithinSector` (compute / filter / role classifier / z-score byte-unchanged — same 26 compute tests pass identically). Zero touch to momentum / reversal / short-interest. Zero touch to MIG-077 / `signal_registry` / `job_registry` row (cron stays disarmed). Zero touch to RBAC / `audit_logs` / SignalsHubPage / AllSignalsTab. No new env vars / npm deps / migrations. |

| **Future use** | The market-wide-fetch-by-date + group-by-ticker pattern becomes the template for the remaining feed-signals (#1 analyst, #2 PEAD, #3 options, #8 news). Any per-ticker-across-839 fetch would hit the same CPU wall. |

### ACT-158: FP-043 PARK — Signal #3 deferred to DEC-047 queue-worker rebuild (DW-095)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ID** | ACT-158 (next-free after ACT-157; grep-verified at HEAD `a800b51`). |
| **Mode** | documentation + small live-DB status update (no code change, no schema change). |
| **Tier** | A — governance. Architecture decision + deferred-work registration + truth-in-telemetry registry flip. |
| **Branch** | feature/FP-043-park-signal-3-dec-047-dw-095. |
| **HEAD before / after** | before: `a800b51` (FP-043 Phase 4 + MIG-079 cadence-truth fix) / after: pending at execution commit. |
| **Authority** | Operator decision 2026-06-09 (PARK Option B chosen over BUILD Option A or INVESTIGATE Option C, in the A/B/C sequencing call following the ACT-157-coordinator investigation). Architecture authority: DEC-047 (Option D cursor-drain queue-worker, formally recorded in this PR). |
| **Scope** | (a) `docs/08-planning/approved-decisions.md` — append DEC-047 with the full Option-D design preserved verbatim (the 493s math, B/E rejections, locked Pro tier input, table topology, cron topology, finalizer barrier, orphan-sweep, heartbeat CAS, reuse list). (b) `docs/08-planning/deferred-work-register.md` — append DW-095 with re-entry trigger + the vendor-shape audit gate. (c) `docs/06-tracking/action-tracker.md` — this entry. (d) `signal_registry.options_flow_imbalance_5d` row — flip `status='live'→'planned'`, rewrite `cadence` to a deferred-equivalent string referencing DW-095/DEC-047 (so the All-Signals UI does not advertise an EOD cadence for a non-running signal), set `planned_phase='DW-095 (queue-worker rebuild; FP-043 fetcher/compute/worker/z-score reused)'`. (e) `docs/07-reference/database-migration-ledger.md` — MIG-080 entry (data-only UPDATE, applied via `supabase--insert` like MIG-079). (f) `docs/04-modules/longshort/signals/options-flow.md` — add a "DEFERRED — coordinator rebuild (DW-095 / DEC-047)" header note explaining the 493s rate-bound parking; preserve all existing content. |
| **Out-of-scope guarantees** | (1) Zero code change anywhere under `supabase/functions/` (FP-043 fetcher / compute / worker / z-score / partial-failure-honesty stack untouched — all reused verbatim by the future DW-095 rebuild). (2) Zero schema change (the registry flip is a data UPDATE only; `signal_registry` enum unchanged). (3) `job_registry.longshort.options_flow.compute.enabled` stays `false` (verified post-update: `enabled=false`, `status='registered'`, `schedule='0 22 * * 1-5'`). (4) No new permissions / events / configs / env-vars / cron jobs / edge functions. (5) DEC-045 (Tradier vendor lock) and DEC-046 (v1 conscious approximation) preserved unchanged — the rebuild inherits both. (6) MIG-078 and MIG-079 not reverted — the disarmed job_registry seed remains as the future re-arm target. |
| **Live-DB evidence (§22.5.1)** | Post-flip read of `signal_registry`: `signal_id=options_flow_imbalance_5d`, `status=planned` (was `live`), `cadence='deferred — coordinator rebuild pending (DW-095 / DEC-047 cursor-drain queue-worker; §4.4.7 EOD daily target preserved, 5-min intraday remains v2 per DEC-046)'`, `planned_phase='DW-095 (queue-worker rebuild; FP-043 fetcher/compute/worker/z-score reused)'`, `job_registry_id='longshort.options_flow.compute'` (preserved — references the disarmed seed), `stale_after_hours=72` (preserved — appropriate for the eventual EOD cadence after rebuild), `updated_at=2026-06-09 17:09:11 UTC`. Companion read of `job_registry`: `id=longshort.options_flow.compute`, `enabled=false`, `status=registered`, `schedule='0 22 * * 1-5'` — disarm confirmed. |
| **Why PARK over BUILD (Option A) or INVESTIGATE (Option C)** | Build-once economics + risk profile. Option A (build queue-worker now for Signal #3 alone) commits the heaviest piece of orchestration infra in the signals layer for one non-critical signal, before knowing whether Signals #1/#2/#8 will also need it — risking a v2 rewrite once a second consumer's shape appears. Option B (park, audit, then build #1/#2/#8) lets the vendor-shape audit reveal the real number of queue-worker consumers, so DW-095 lands at full fidelity with all client requirements known. Option C (investigate Option E pre-filter) requires a second vendor procurement decision and a §4.4.7 spec change — orthogonal to the architecture call. Signal #3's compute is verified clean and the architecture is fully designed in DEC-047 (nothing is lost by parking). |
| **ROI Impact** | **Zero on prediction / signal / sizing / execution logic** — no money-path code touched; the FP-043 compute math is preserved verbatim for the rebuild. **Neutral-to-positive on operational truth** — the All-Signals UI now correctly shows Signal #3 as `planned` (deferred) rather than advertising an EOD cadence for a non-running signal; aligns with the truth-in-telemetry discipline that MIG-079 already established for the cadence string. **Negative on Signal #3's operational availability** — but Signal #3 is non-critical (§4.3.5), the combiner imputes its absence (§6.5), and the irreducible 493s floor means no faster path exists at the v1 architecture. |
| **Incidental Findings** | None. |
| **Risks / follow-up** | (a) Re-entry depends on the vendor-shape audit for Signals #1/#2/#8 — explicitly gated in DW-095. (b) The disarmed `job_registry` row remains as a future re-arm target; the broken `runOptionsFlowCoordinator` synchronous Promise.all path remains in the codebase to be replaced wholesale by the DW-095 rebuild — not deleted now to preserve the test fixtures and the reuse contract for `compute-options-flow.ts` / `tradier-options-chain-fetcher.ts` / `token-bucket.ts` / `options-flow-chunk-runner.ts`. (c) If an operator manually fires `longshort-options-flow-compute-manual` against the current shell, it will 504 IDLE_TIMEOUT and write a `failed` `signal_compute_log` row (same telemetry signature as the 2026-06-09 16:42:50 UTC run); the manual handler is not separately disarmed because the cron is disarmed (the only production fire path). Operator awareness: do not curl the manual handler until DW-095 lands. |
| **Verification Status** | DOCUMENTATION-ONLY + data-update verified via live-DB read (above). Gate-4 N/A (no code change). Gate-2 N/A (no code change). Gate-11 N/A (no code change). DEC-047 + DW-095 + this entry + MIG-080 ledger entry + options-flow.md header-note ship in same diff (Rule 6 — same-PR documentation discipline). |
| **Status** | Pending commit. |
